This is a natural choice, but it creates a mismatch: VLMs encode rich, high-level representations of scenes and goals, while robot actions are low-level signals with limited task structure. We ask whether changing what the policy is trained to predict, rather than how it is architecturally designed, can yield better and more efficiently trained policies. We propose UVT (Unified Visuomotor Target), a unified latent prediction target that jointly encodes motor control and visual scene transition information, requiring no architectural changes and no additional data. Applied to two representative VLA systems across simulation benchmarks and real bimanual manipulation tasks, UVT improves training efficiency, final task performance, and policy robustness, with particularly strong gains under limited training budgets and challenging environmental conditions. Rollout videos and additional qualitative results are available at our project webpage: https://unified-visuomotor-targets.github.io/

</details>


### [368] [Human Centric Embodied Intelligence for Soft Wearable Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03556)
*Rainier Natividad,Raye Chen-Hua Yeow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出以人为中心的具身智能（HCEI）概念及感知-认知-行动-增强（PCAA）框架，综述软体可穿戴机器人在材料、传感、智能、驱动、交互、临床转化等方面的进展，强调从“致动器优先”转向“感知与认知驱动”的设计范式，以推动个性化、预测性和人本化的可穿戴智能系统发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体可穿戴机器人领域日趋成熟，核心挑战从开发更软的材料和更强的致动器，转向如何将感知、智能和人体适应整合到可长期穿戴、可信赖且有益的系统中。现有研究缺乏统一框架来指导这种跨学科整合，因此需要提出新概念和组织视角。

Method: 通过综述方法，提出HCEI概念和PCAA框架，并基于此框架系统梳理软材料、可穿戴传感、人工智能、驱动、人机交互、数字孪生、临床转化、制造、法规和伦理等关键方向的研究进展与相互关联。

Result: 归纳了各技术在软体可穿戴机器人中的最新进展，阐明了感知与认知作为设计核心的重要性，展示了多组件协同如何影响长期个性化和实际部署，并提供了统一的框架来促进领域整合。

Conclusion: PCAA框架为软体可穿戴机器人研究提供了新的设计视角，有助于指导未来研究、促进跨学科合作，并加速向个性化、预测性和以人为中心的下一代可穿戴智能系统转化。

Abstract: Soft wearable robots have evolved rapidly from proof-of-concept devices into promising platforms for rehabilitation, occupational assistance, and human augmentation. As the field matures, its central challenge extends beyond the development of softer materials and more capable actuators to the integration of sensing, intelligence, and human adaptation into systems that users can wear comfortably, trust, and benefit from over extended periods. This transition motivates the concept of Human-Centric Embodied Intelligence (HCEI), in which intelligence emerges from the coupled human-robot system through the interaction of morphology, multimodal sensing, adaptive cognition, compliant actuation, and the wearer's own physiological and behavioral adaptation. To organize this perspective, this review introduces the Perception-Cognition-Actuation-Augmentation (PCAA) framework, which positions perception and cognition as the primary drivers of design, shifting development beyond the conventional actuator-first paradigm. Using this framework, the review synthesizes advances in soft materials, wearable sensing, artificial intelligence, actuation, human-robot interaction, digital twins, clinical translation, manufacturing, regulation, and ethics, highlighting how these interdependent components collectively shape long-term personalization and real-world deployment. By providing a unified conceptual framework and design perspective, this review aims to guide future research, foster interdisciplinary collaboration, and accelerate the translation of next-generation soft wearable robots toward personalized, predictive, and human-centric wearable intelligence.

</details>


### [369] [LiLa-WAM: Lightweight Latent Reasoning World-Action Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03701)
*Fan Yang,Yuting Su,Xiaobo Wang,Yuncheng You,Fugui Fan,Yuting Wu,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,JiaHong Ning,Peiguang Jing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级世界动作模型LiLa-WAM，在紧凑潜在空间中推理未来，并能在单个24GB GPU上端到端训练，且性能优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型计算开销大，像素空间方法关注无关视觉细节，潜在空间方法需要多阶段训练，导致训练成本高，难以在有限算力下使用。

Method: 设计紧凑潜在推理空间，通过未来状态预测和动作生成联合塑造；提出无语言任务表示Visual Transition Token (VTT)，将任务编码为视觉特征空间中的方向。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO和真实机器人任务上，LiLa-WAM在50个RoboTwin任务中达到90.48%的成功率，且仅需单GPU训练。

Conclusion: LiLa-WAM证明轻量级世界动作模型可以在低算力下高效训练并保持高性能，VTT作为一种有效的任务表示方式可行。

Abstract: World-action modeling has emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic control, as it empowers models to go beyond reacting to observations and anticipate how a scene will evolve. However, existing WAMs often incur substantial computational overhead. Pixel-space methods often allocate substantial capacity to visual details that may not be directly relevant to control, while some latent-space methods require multi-stage training to construct the reasoning space. The resulting training cost can make such methods difficult to train under modest computational budgets. In this work, we propose LiLa-WAM, a lightweight world-action model that reasons about the future in a compact latent space and can be trained end-to-end on a single 24GB GPU. Its core design is a compact latent reasoning space jointly shaped by future-state prediction and action generation, which keeps the model lightweight while remaining well aligned with control. For task specification, we further propose the Visual Transition Token(VTT), a language-free task representation that encodes each task as a direction in visual feature space. Experiments on RoboTwin~2.0, LIBERO, and real-robot tasks demonstrate LiLa-WAM's effectiveness, achieving 90.48\% success across 50 RoboTwin tasks with single-GPU training.

</details>


### [370] [Track4Action: Distilling World-Centric 3D Tracker into Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03727)
*Chenyi Wang,Xinkai Wang,Bokai Lin,Jialin Tian,Fucheng Zhang,Cewu Lu,Lixin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Track4Action框架，通过将世界中心3D跟踪器从演示片段中提取的转移特征蒸馏到当前观测VLA策略中，提升动作对齐能力，无需在部署时使用跟踪器或片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作标签只告诉VLA策略模仿哪些命令，未提供命令如何改变3D世界的信息，而演示片段中的帧转移包含了这种监督信号。

Method: 训练时用世界中心3D跟踪器编码片段为池化跟踪特征，可学习的跟踪查询从VLA隐藏状态推断该特征，在共享空间匹配，并通过特征门控条件化流匹配动作头。部署时仅使用当前观测。

Result: 零样本LIBERO-Plus达82.3%，比无对齐变体高7.6点，比LaMP高3.0点；RoboTwin 2.0 clean和randomized分割分别达80.44%和81.48%；物理双臂任务平均成功率67.5%，比无对齐变体高25.0点。

Conclusion: 动作对齐的3D跟踪特征可作为特权监督，提升无跟踪器部署的VLA策略性能，在仿真和物理任务中均有效。

Abstract: Action labels tell a vision-language-action (VLA) policy which robot commands to imitate, but not how those commands change the 3D world. The aligned demonstration clip contains this missing supervision because its $K$ frame transitions record the geometry, motion, visibility, and camera change produced during the corresponding $K$ actions. We introduce Track4Action, a framework that distills this realized transition from a frozen world-centric 3D tracker into a current-observation VLA policy. During training, Track4World encodes the clip $V_{t:t+K}$ into a pooled tracker feature. Learnable track queries infer this feature from current VLA hidden states, match it in a shared space, and condition a flow-matching action head through a feature-wise gate. The tracker feature only defines the alignment target, so neither the clip nor the tracker is used at deployment. Track4Action reaches 82.3% on zero-shot LIBERO-Plus, improving the alignment-free variant by 7.6 points and LaMP by 3.0 points. It obtains 80.44% and 81.48% on the clean and randomized RoboTwin 2.0 splits, and 67.5% average success across four physical bimanual tasks, 25.0 points above the alignment-free variant. The gains across simulation and physical tasks support action-aligned 3D tracker features as privileged supervision for tracker-free VLA deployment. Our project page is available at https://wing0night.github.io/track4action-project-page.

</details>


### [371] [GORDON: Graph-based Object-centric Rewards for Decomposition of Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03753)
*Andrea Protopapa,Davide Buoso,Francesca Pistilli,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Giuseppe Averta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GORDON，一种基于图的物体中心奖励学习框架，从无动作视频演示中学习稠密奖励，并通过自动子任务发现实现长时程操作技能学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作技能强化学习面临奖励设计复杂、稀疏奖励指导有限、手动子任务标注成本高等挑战，而基于像素的奖励学习对视觉变化敏感。

Method: 将每个视觉场景表示为检测物体和空间关系的图，用图神经网络自监督嵌入到任务对齐的潜在空间，引入活动感知加权池化强调任务相关物体，计算当前状态与示范目标配置在潜在空间的距离作为稠密奖励，并通过奖励的时间剖面自动发现子任务，训练子任务特定奖励和策略。

Result: 在MAGICAL和ManiSkill3基准的七个操作任务上，物体中心奖励提升了短时程强化学习性能，并在长时程任务中通过自动分解实现成功策略学习，平均成功率74.4%，比最佳学习基线高约35个百分点，比oracle高约25个百分点。

Conclusion: GORDON通过物体中心图表示和自动子任务发现，有效解决长时程操作技能学习中的奖励设计和任务分解问题。

Abstract: Learning long-horizon manipulation skills with reinforcement learning remains challenging due to the complexity of reward design, the limited guidance of sparse rewards, and the high cost of manual subtask annotation. Visual demonstrations can provide supervision for reward learning, but rewards learned from raw pixels can be brittle and sensitive to visual variation, background appearance, and robot motion. In this work, we propose GORDON, a graph-based object-centric reward learning framework that learns dense rewards from action-free video demonstrations. Each visual scene is represented as a graph of detected objects and spatial relations, and a graph neural network is trained in a self-supervised manner to embed these graphs into a task-aligned latent space. To align the representation with semantic task progress, we introduce an activity-aware weighted pooling mechanism that emphasizes task-relevant objects while masking robot-dominated motion. The dense reward is then computed as distances in the learned latent space of the current state to demonstrated goal configurations, providing a measure of task progress. In long-horizon tasks, the temporal profile of this reward reveals stage-wise object-state transitions, enabling automatic subtask discovery without manual segmentation. The discovered segments are then used to train subtask-specific rewards and specialized policies that are composed sequentially. Experiments on seven manipulation tasks on MAGICAL and ManiSkill3 benchmarks show that our object-centric reward improves reinforcement learning in short-horizon settings and enables successful policy learning in complex long-horizon tasks through automatic decomposition, achieving an average success rate of 74.4% across the long-horizon tasks (on average approximately +35 p.p. vs. best learned baseline and approximately +25 p.p. vs. oracle).

</details>


### [372] [Design and Evaluation of an AI-Enabled Cloud-Edge Architecture for Connected Precision Agriculture Farms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03816)
*Deckshitha Angadi,Koteshwar Goud Surga,Nagaraju Lakkaraju,Naveena Budda,Vikas Agarwal,Giridhara Venkata Ram Raj Mulasa,Ravi Killamsetty,Chandrasekhara Sarma Mallubhotla,Narsimlu Kemsaram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于AI的云边架构，集成IoT、无人机、深度学习和Azure云分析，实现番茄病害的实时检测，准确率约92-95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工监测在大规模农场中效率低且难以管理，番茄病害导致严重减产，需要自动化的作物监测系统。

Method: 构建云边架构，结合IoT传感器、无人机、深度学习模型（TensorFlow），部署于移动、网页和边缘设备平台，使用公开数据集训练。

Result: 检测准确率约92-95%，在不同环境和平台上表现一致，降低了人工依赖并支持及时干预。

Conclusion: 所提系统能有效检测番茄病害，支持可持续的精准农业，促进智慧农场发展。

Abstract: Plant diseases cause significant yield losses worldwide, with tomato crops particularly susceptible to early blight, late blight, and leaf mold. Manual monitoring is practical only for small-scale farms and becomes unmanageable at larger scales. To tackle this limitation, an artificial intelligence (AI) enabled cloud-edge architecture is proposed for autonomous crop monitoring. This proposed architecture integrates Internet of Things (IoT) sensors, unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), deep learning, Azure IoT Hub-based cloud analytics, and multi-platform (mobile app, web app, and embedded edge device platform) interfaces to enable real-time detection of tomato diseases. For training and validation, we used publicly available datasets, such as PlantVillage and Kaggle. A TensorFlow model trained on a collected dataset is deployed across mobile, web, and edge-device platforms. Experimental results show detection effectiveness around 92-95%, with consistent performance over diverse environments and device platforms. The proposed system improves disease detection effectiveness, lowers dependence on manual inspection, and enables prompt interventions, thereby supporting sustainable, connected precision agriculture farms.

</details>


### [373] [Designing Social Robots for Inclusive Child Wellbeing Assessment: Insights from Communities Supporting Developmental Language Disorder and Forced Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03820)
*Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Yue Lou,Alva Markelius,Emma Geijer-Simpson,Gustaf Gredebäck,Tamsin Jane Ford,Ginevra Castellano,Hatice Gunes,Georgina Warner,Jenny L. Gibson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过焦点小组探讨了如何为不同沟通需求的儿童（如发展性语言障碍和被迫移民背景）设计包容且合乎伦理的社交机器人辅助幸福感评估，并提出了设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自我报告问卷对语言理解和表达要求高，难以适用于有沟通障碍的儿童，而社交机器人虽有潜力但相关包容性设计研究不足。

Method: 创建候选的儿童-机器人互动活动作为设计探针，并组织家长和专业人士的焦点小组，通过主题分析提炼关键考虑因素。

Result: 确定了机器人角色与能力、互动动态、个体差异、儿童能动性等通用考虑，以及因沟通需求和经历而异的人群特定考虑，最终形成设计建议。

Conclusion: 研究为面向不同沟通需求儿童的机器人介导幸福感评估提供了包容性和伦理性的设计指导。

Abstract: Assessing children's wellbeing and mental health can be particularly challenging for children experiencing communication barriers, such as children with Developmental Language Disorder (DLD) and children with forced migration backgrounds. During the assessment process, traditional self-report questionnaires place substantial demands on language comprehension and verbal expression. In this context, social robots have emerged as a promising tool for supporting wellbeing assessment without solely relying on self-report questionnaires, yet limited research has examined how such interactions can be designed to be inclusive, appropriate, and ethically acceptable for children with diverse communication needs. To address this gap, we created candidate child--robot interaction activities as design probes and conducted focus groups with parents and professionals supporting children with DLD and children with forced migration backgrounds. Through thematic analysis, we identified considerations relating to robot role and capabilities, interactional dynamics, individual differences, and child agency, alongside population-specific considerations shaped by children's communication needs and lived experiences. Based on these findings, we derive a set of ethical and inclusive design recommendations for robot-mediated wellbeing assessment. By foregrounding these considerations and recommendations, this work contributes design guidance for inclusive robot-mediated wellbeing assessments for children with diverse communication needs.

</details>


### [374] [EvoHIL: Self-Evolving Reward and Flow-Matched Policy Optimization for Robust Human-in-the-Loop Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03872)
*Shuoqin Zhang,Tongtong Cheng,Xiru Gao,Jinzhuo Peng,Bin Zheng,Jiahao Tu,Ke Wang,Jia Pan,Zhe Hu,Kai Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EvoHIL是一个统一的框架，通过自进化奖励、动作流稳定和保留感知的离线微调，在人类参与循环的强化学习中适应奖励模型、动作生成器和视觉领域，从而在光照变化下提高机器人的操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人类参与循环强化学习（HIL-RL）方法在部署时面临三个耦合限制：静态视觉奖励模型无法适应场景变化、独立采样的动作导致时间不一致的运动、视觉策略对外观变化敏感。这些限制阻碍了机器人从有限的真实交互中学习接触丰富的操作任务。

Method: EvoHIL包含三个关键组件：1）自进化奖励（SER）通过人类确认的正样本和临时弱负样本自适应地更新成功分类器；2）动作流稳定（AFS）通过流匹配生成时间上连贯的动作块，将策略更新锚定在执行的动作前缀和示范行为上；3）保留感知的离线微调通过重放重新照明后的交互数据，同时锚定AFS演员-评论家到先前的行为，无需额外机器人交互即可适应视觉领域。

Result: 在Franka FR3和SO-101机械臂的六个操作任务中，在受控光照变化下，EvoHIL相对人类参与循环和模仿基线改善了任务成功率、与人类确认标签的一致性、运动平滑度和完成时间。

Conclusion: EvoHIL有效解决了HIL-RL中的三个关键限制，通过自适应奖励、时间一致的动作生成和视觉领域适应，显著提升了机器人在变化环境中的操作性能，展示了在实际应用中适应场景变化的潜力。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning (HIL-RL) enables robots to learn contact-rich manipulation from limited real-world interaction, but deployment exposes three coupled limitations: static visual reward models fail under scene changes; independently sampled actions cause temporally inconsistent motion; and vision-based policies remain sensitive to appearance shifts. We present EvoHIL, a unified framework that adapts the reward model, action generator, and visual do main within a staged human-in-the-loop learning process. First, self-evolving reward (SER) adapts the success classifier from human-confirmed positives and provisional weak negatives. Second, Action Flow Stabilization (AFS) generates temporally coherent action chunks through flow matching, grounding policy updates in executed action prefixes and demonstrated behavior. Third, retention-aware offline fine-tuning replays relit interaction data while anchoring the AFS actor-critic to prior behavior, adapting the visual domain without additional robot interaction. Across six manipulation tasks on Franka FR3 and SO-101 arms under a controlled lighting shift, EvoHIL improves task success, agreement with human-confirmation labels, motion smoothness, and completion time relative to human-in-the-loop and imitation baselines.Project page: https://anonymous4366.github.io/EvoHIL/

</details>


### [375] [ETA: A New Agentic Paradigm for Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03924)
*Yitong Chen,Zezheng Huai,Sixian Li,Yubang Wang,Haozhe Zhang,Yifei Zhang,Hechang Chen,Jingjing Gong,Yu-Gang Jiang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Embodied Task Agent (ETA)的新范式，通过将数字代理扩展到物理世界，实现通用机器人的目标。ETA围绕规划器、接口和世界循环运作，使代理能够验证结果、调整计划并积累经验。同时发布了其开源实现OpenETA，支持可替换的规划器、可组合的工具和技能、可审计的记忆以及可重放的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身系统采用端到端的观察-行动路径，泛化能力依赖于训练数据的覆盖，且长任务执行难以控制和检查。为了推动机器人技术达到类似ChatGPT的突破，需要一种通用机器人，能够处理不熟悉的任务和环境，保持长期交互的可控性，并从经验中学习。

Method: ETA范式将机器人的操作分为三个核心组件：规划器（Planner）每次选择一个工具调用，接口（Interface）控制执行，世界（World）返回结果和新观察。这个循环使代理能够验证结果并调整计划，同时将成功和失败的交互转化为可复用的经验。OpenETA作为开源实现，提供了可替换的规划器、可组合的工具和技能、可审计的记忆、可重放的轨迹，以及模拟和真实机器人的通用接口。

Result: 通过OpenETA，机器人可以操作多种规划器，如Codex，并作为轻量级插件暴露简单的接口（observe、mark_point、move_to），从而支持各种任务的实际执行和持续学习。

Conclusion: ETA范式为机器人提供了一种新的发展路径，强调交互循环和经验积累，有望推动机器人向通用化发展，并实现更可控和可扩展的具身智能。

Abstract: When will robots have their ChatGPT moment? Such a breakthrough requires a general-purpose robot that can handle unfamiliar tasks in unfamiliar environments, remain controllable over long interactions, and learn from experience.
  Today's embodied systems largely follow an end-to-end observation-to-action path. Despite rapid progress, they remain far from this goal: their generalization depends heavily on the coverage of robot training data, while long task execution remains difficult to control and inspect. To realize this goal, we introduce the Embodied Task Agent (ETA), a new paradigm for extending digital agents into the physical world, and release OpenETA as its open-source implementation. ETA centers the robot around a Planner that chooses one Tool call at a time, an Interface that controls execution, and a World that returns the result and a fresh observation. This loop allows the agent to verify outcomes, adapt its plan, and turn successful and failed interactions into reusable experience. OpenETA provides replaceable Planners, composable Tools and Skills, auditable memory, replayable trajectories, and common interfaces for simulation and real robots. For Codex, OpenETA can operate as a lightweight plugin that exposes only observe, mark_point, and move_to.

</details>


### [376] [Stochastic Multiple Shooting Trajectory Optimization via Sequential Local Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03978)
*Ashwin Gupta,Joseph Moore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种随机多射击轨迹优化方法，通过短控制序列与局部反馈策略连接，提高终端约束满足的样本效率与收敛性，并可从卷积分合成近似雅可比矩阵，适用于黑箱动力学。在三个欠驱动非线性问题上验证了改进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机单射击轨迹优化（如MPPI）在处理长序列时终端约束满足样本效率低、收敛慢，需要大量迭代。

Method: 提出随机多射击方法，优化短控制序列并通过局部反馈策略连接；利用卷积分合成近似系统雅可比矩阵，支持黑箱动力学的模型强化学习。

Result: 在三个非线性欠驱动问题（经典cartpole摆动、神经网络动力学cartpole摆动、VTOL四翼飞机高攻角失速着陆）上，该算法显著提升了样本效率和终端集收敛性。

Conclusion: 随机多射击方法结合局部反馈和近似雅可比，有效改善长序列优化下的样本效率与终端约束收敛，适合黑箱动力学场景。

Abstract: Stochastic single shooting trajectory optimization methods such as Model Predictive Path Integral control (MPPI) have been widely adopted in robotics due to their ability to reason about probabilistic dynamics and provide solutions where model gradients are noisy, costly to evaluate, or unavailable. However, satisfaction of terminal constraints when shooting over long action sequences is often sample inefficient, requiring a large number of iterations for convergence. In this paper, we present a stochastic multiple shooting method that optimizes short control action sequences connected via local feedback policies to improve sample efficiency and convergence to a terminal set. Additionally, we show that we are able to synthesize approximate system Jacobians purely from rollouts, making the method suitable for model-based reinforcement learning with black-box dynamics. We demonstrate the algorithm has improved sample efficiency and terminal set convergence for three nonlinear, underactuated optimization problems: a classic cartpole swingup task with analytical dynamics, a cartpole swingup task with learned neural network dynamics, and a VTOL quadplane performing a high angle-of-attack, precision post-stall landing maneuver.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [377] [Sequential Operational Decision-Making for Power System Resilience Under Evolving Wildfires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02976)
*Arastoo H Salimi,Majid Dehghani,Hamidreza Nazaripouya*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动化决策支持框架，通过随机多阶段规划来增强电力系统在野火下的韧性，综合考虑预防与校正措施，并利用新算法和随机对偶动态规划求解，在IEEE 30节点和300节点系统上验证了其有效性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火对电力系统构成严重威胁，而现有方法多为单阶段优化，无法适应野火动态演变，因而需要一种自动化、适应性强的决策框架来提升系统韧性，降低风险、成本和负荷削减。

Method: 将决策过程建模为随机多阶段规划，包含预防和校正行动；提出基于野火蔓延和地理信息的决策树构建算法，并采用新颖的随机对偶动态规划方法求解全局最优。

Result: 在IEEE 30节点系统上验证了不同野火场景下的效果，并在IEEE 300节点系统上展示了可扩展性；结果表明该框架优于单阶段优化策略，显著增强了野火条件下的电网韧性。

Conclusion: 所提自动化框架能有效提升电力系统在野火下的韧性，具有实际应用价值，且能适应野火动态变化。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel automated decision-support framework aimed at enhancing the resilience of power systems and operational resilience against wildfires by formulating the decision-making process as a stochastic multi-stage programming during a progressive wildfire. The paper develops a framework that takes into account both preventive and corrective actions, enabling automated and adaptive decisions based on potential scenarios over the course of a wildfire's progression. This approach considers the evolving nature of the wildfire threat and seeks to optimize the response strategies accordingly throughout its duration. The objective is to minimize wildfire risk and operational costs while reducing load curtailment. The framework accounts for potential contingencies caused by progressive wildfires. A novel algorithm is proposed to construct a decision tree based on wildfire progression and system geographical information. Additionally, a novel stochastic dual dynamic programming approach is deployed to solve the proposed optimization problem, achieving a global optimum for the framework. The effectiveness of the proposed method is demonstrated on the IEEE 30-bus system under various wildfire impact scenarios and is then applied to the IEEE 300-bus system to illustrate the scalability of the proposed approach. The results highlight the advantages of the proposed automated framework over a single-stage operational optimization strategy in enhancing power grid resilience under wildfire conditions.

</details>


### [378] [Enhancing Operational Grid Resilience Against Wildfires Under Decision-Dependent Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02978)
*Arastoo H Salimi,Hamidreza Nazaripouya*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑决策依赖不确定性的自动化决策框架，通过预防和纠正措施增强电力系统应对野火的韧性，并在IEEE 30节点系统上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火对电力系统构成严重威胁，现有方法未充分考虑早期决策（如公共安全断电）对后续野火场景概率的影响，导致决策不现实或韧性不足。

Method: 构建多阶段优化模型，融入决策依赖不确定性，并采用数学分解算法高效求解。

Result: 在IEEE 30节点系统上的案例研究表明，考虑DDU能提供更现实且具有运营韧性的解决方案。

Conclusion: 所提框架能提升电力系统在野火演变中的自适应决策能力，增强系统韧性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new automated decision-making framework to enhance the resilience of electrical systems against wildfires by applying operational strategies that account for decision-dependent uncertainty (DDU). The proposed framework incorporates both preventive and corrective measures, enabling adaptive and automated decision-making throughout the course of evolving wildfire scenarios. First, a baseline multistage optimization model is presented as a foundation to support wildfire-driven operational decision-making. The model then incorporates DDU, wherein Public Safety Power Shutoff (PSPS) decisions made in earlier stages influence the probabilities and parameters of future wildfire scenarios. To efficiently solve the resulting complex optimization problem, a mathematical decomposition algorithm is employed. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is demonstrated through case studies on the IEEE 30-bus system. Simulation results confirm that incorporating the impact of DDU into the optimization process provides more realistic, and operationally resilient solutions in the face of evolving wildfire threats.

</details>


### [379] [Process-Knowledge-Embedded Safe DRL for Real-Time Dispatch of Process Loads in Industrial Microgrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03149)
*Daniyaer Paizulamu,Lin Cheng,Fashun Shi,Yuchi Zhang,Zhaoyang Dong*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种过程知识嵌入的安全深度强化学习框架，用于工业微电网中炼钢负荷的实时调度，在保证过程可行性的同时降低用电成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度强化学习难以在减少成本的同时维持生产过程可行性，因为炼钢过程具有强多级耦合特性，当前决策会影响后续可行性。

Method: 构建无损主动前沿动作空间，设计基于过程距离的动作处理机制，建立递归过程可行性保证，并将预期过程修正距离融入PPO算法（通过修正预算和原始-对偶更新），同时推导出原始策略对安全处理的依赖界限。

Result: 基于实际数据的案例研究表明，该方法实现零过程损失，相比基于规则的调度和滚动MILP，用电成本分别降低49.2%和25.9%，且计算时间可接受。

Conclusion: 所提出框架能有效平衡经济性与过程可行性，为工业微电网中复杂过程负荷的实时调度提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Steelmaking process loads (SPLs) are flexible resources that enhance local renewable-energy utilization and reduce electricity procurement costs in industrial microgrids. However, strong multistage coupling makes current decisions affect subsequent feasibility, challenging conventional deep reinforcement learning to reduce costs while maintaining process feasibility throughout production. This paper proposes a process-knowledge-embedded safe deep reinforcement learning framework for the real-time dispatch of SPLs in industrial microgrids. Specifically, a lossless active-frontier action space is constructed, and a process-distance-guided action-processing mechanism reallocates excluded-action probabilities according to process distance and the actor's safe-action preference. Recursive process feasibility is established to guarantee admissible execution and feasible continuation. Furthermore, the expected process-correction distance is incorporated into PPO through a correction budget and a primal-dual update to internalize process knowledge into the raw policy, while a derived bound quantifies the raw policy's dependence on safety processing. Case studies using real-world data demonstrate zero process losses, electricity-cost reductions of 49.2% and 25.9% relative to rule-based scheduling and rolling MILP, respectively, within an acceptable computation time.

</details>


### [380] [Calibration of a Macroscopic Coupled People-Epidemic Transport PDE Model via Density and Velocity Computation from Microscopic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03157)
*Nikoletta Hadjihabi,Nikolaos Bekiaris-Liberis*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从足球场微观轨迹数据推导和平滑宏观密度与速度的方法，并构建人群流动-疫情传播PDE模型进行数值求解和校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了从微观个体位置数据中提取宏观人群动态，并量化疫情在大型集会中的传播风险。

Method: 基于个体轨迹计算易感、感染和暴露个体的宏观密度，通过关键接触时长识别暴露者；采用有限体积法数值求解耦合的人群流动-疫情传播偏微分方程，并用平滑后的密度进行模型校准。

Result: 成功得到平滑的宏观密度场，并完成PDE模型的数值模拟与参数校准。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效从微观数据中构建宏观模型，为大型场所的疫情传播预测提供工具。

Abstract: We introduce an approach for derivation and smoothing of macroscopic densities and velocities from people trajectories, obtained from microscopic position data of individuals within a football stadium. We compute macroscopic densities specifically for susceptible, infected, and exposed individuals, via detection of exposed individuals based on the duration of critical contacts between susceptible and infected. We then present and numerically solve a crowd flow - epidemic spreading PDE model using a finite volume scheme. Finally, we calibrate the model using the smoothed densities computed.

</details>


### [381] [GriD-LMIA: A Gridding-Based Assembler for Solving Differentiable Parameter-Dependent Linear Matrix Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03175)
*Yicheng Xu,Faryar Jabbari*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为GriD-LMIA的MATLAB工具包，用于将连续域上的参数相关线性矩阵不等式（PD-LMIs）转换为有限个充分LMIs，通过网格划分和Tensor Bernstein多项式表示，并提供了多种证书选项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PD-LMIs需要在连续域上成立，而半定规划求解器只能处理有限约束。当调度参数随时间变化时，还可能需要参数相关决策变量的导数条件，这增加了求解难度。因此，需要一种方法将连续条件离散化为有限个可求解的LMI。

Method: 该方法首先用超矩形网格划分连续域，在每个单元格上用Tensor Bernstein多项式表示已知数据和决策变量。然后，通过导出的直接Bernstein、Pólya提升和平方和（SOS）证书，利用YALMIP生成有限个充分LMIs。

Result: 通过示例检验了网格密度、决策多项式阶数和证书选择之间的平衡，表明该工具包能够有效处理PD-LMI问题，并提供了不同的精度和计算成本权衡。

Conclusion: GriD-LMIA为处理连续域上的PD-LMIs提供了一种灵活且实用的工具，允许用户根据需求调整网格和证书类型，从而在求解精度和效率之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Parameter-dependent linear matrix inequalities (PD-LMIs) require holding over a continuous domain. When the scheduling parameters vary with time, derivatives of parameter-dependent decisions may also enter the conditions. Since semidefinite programming solvers require finitely many constraints, we introduce GriD-LMIA, the Gridding-based Differentiable PD-LMI Assembler. It converts the conditions that need to hold on a continuous domain into finitely many sufficient LMIs in MATLAB. It first partitions the domain with a hyper-rectangular grid and represents known data and decisions on each cell with tensor Bernstein polynomials. The package exports direct Bernstein, Pólya-elevated, and sum-of-squares-based certificates through YALMIP. Examples are given to examine the balance among grid density, decision degree, and certificate choice.

</details>


### [382] [Dynamic Flexibility Requests in Local Flexibility Markets: Quantifying the DSO Willingness to Pay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03226)
*Savvas Panagi,Chrysovalantis Spanias,Petros Aristidou*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种动态灵活性需求方法，将配电系统运营商（DSO）的支付意愿嵌入市场出清目标，通过货币化变压器/电缆老化、网络损耗和电压阻塞，并利用凸分段线性外逼近实现高效求解，在改进的CIGRE中压基准上验证了其相比现有方法能提高市场流动性、采购效率和网络运行水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有本地灵活性市场（LFM）出清方法要么依赖不切实际的集中式AC-OPF算法，要么采用基于拥塞管理的静态灵活性需求，忽视了灵活性的经济价值，导致市场效率低下。

Method: 提出动态灵活性需求方法，将DSO的支付意愿（通过货币化老化、损耗和阻塞）嵌入市场出清目标；开发并证明精确的凸分段线性外逼近（epigraph）重新表述，用于IEEE C57.91变压器老化模型和基于Arrhenius的电缆老化模型，以实现计算高效的出清。

Result: 在改进的CIGRE中压基准上验证，与最新方法相比，显著提高了市场流动性、灵活性采购效率和网络运行水平，同时保持市场的透明性和非歧视原则。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够更高效地采购灵活性，并改善网络运行，为DSO在本地灵活性市场中提供了实用且经济可行的出清机制。

Abstract: Local Flexibility Markets (LFMs) require Distribution System Operators (DSOs) to determine both the quantity of flexibility to procure and the corresponding willingness to pay during market clearing. Existing approaches typically rely on unrealistic centralized AC-OPF clearing algorithms or strictly localized, static flexibility requests driven primarily by congestion management, while the economic value of flexibility is largely neglected. This paper proposes a dynamic flexibility-request methodology in which the DSO's willingness to pay is embedded directly into the market-clearing objective by monetizing transformer and cable aging, network losses, and voltage congestion. To enable computationally efficient clearing, exact convex piecewise-linear epigraph reformulations of the IEEE C57.91 transformer aging model and an Arrhenius-based cable aging model are developed and formally proven to preserve exactness. The proposed framework is validated on a modified CIGRE MV benchmark and compared with a recent state-of-the-art flexibility-request methodology. The results demonstrate significantly higher market liquidity, more efficient flexibility procurement, and improved network operations while preserving the market's transparency and non-discrimination principles.

</details>


### [383] [Partitioned Mixed Small Gain-Phase Decentralized Stability Criterion for Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03641)
*Diego Cifelli,Adolfo Anta*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分区混合增益-相位分散稳定性判据，允许不同转换器子集在同一频率下满足不同的局部要求（小增益或小相位），从而减少保守性，并在异构电力系统中验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分散混合小增益和小相位判据要求所有转换器在同一频率下满足相同类型条件，无法同时利用构网型转换器的相位特性和构网型转换器的低增益特性，导致不必要的保守性。

Method: 提出分区混合增益-相位分散稳定性判据，允许不同子集分别通过小增益或小相位条件进行认证，并通过网络相关的二次约束确定增益和相位裕度之间的可接受权衡，同时分析可接受集合的凸性和有界性，并给出实际选择流程。

Result: 在包含构网型和构网型转换器的异构系统（两转换器系统和IEEE 39节点系统）中，该方法优于标准分散小增益和小相位条件，验证了其有效性和减少保守性的优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的分区混合判据为异构电力系统提供了一种更灵活、保守性更低的稳定性认证方法，能够根据转换器的不同特性进行技术感知的稳定性分析。

Abstract: The increasing penetration of converter-interfaced resources is making power-system stability assessment more challenging, particularly in heterogeneous grids containing both grid-forming and grid-following converters. Existing decentralized mixed small-gain and small-phase criteria provide scalable stability certificates, but they require all converters to satisfy the same type of condition at a given frequency. As a result, they cannot simultaneously exploit the low gain of grid-following converters and the favorable phase properties of grid-forming converters, leading to unnecessary conservatism. This paper proposes a partitioned mixed gain-phase decentralized stability criterion that allows distinct subsets of converters to satisfy different local requirements at the same frequency. Specifically, one subset can be certified through small-gain bounds, while the complementary subset is certified through small-phase bounds. The admissible trade-off between gain and phase margins is determined by a network-dependent quadratic constraint, yielding a technology-aware stability certificate that remains local at the converter level. The paper characterizes the admissible set of gain and phase bounds, establishes useful convexity and boundedness properties, and develops a practical procedure for selecting these bounds. The proposed method is demonstrated on heterogeneous systems containing grid-forming and grid-following converters, including a two-converter system and the IEEE 39-bus system, outperforming the standard decentralized small-gain and small-phase conditions.

</details>


### [384] [Precision Specimen Positioning in Electron Microscopy through Hysteresis Compensation, Iterative Learning, and Vision-Based Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03669)
*J. S. van Hulst,A. M. C. de Peffer,D. Herceg,E. M. Franken,E. Verschueren,W. P. M. H. Heemels,D. J. Antunes*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成前馈框架，用于在操作中的电子显微镜内实现长行程纳米级样品定位，通过迟滞补偿、基于图像的POI测量和迭代学习控制，将跟踪误差降低7到13倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子显微镜需要长行程纳米级定位，压电步进执行器适用但受迟滞、机械未对准和非共置传感限制。先前方法仅在简化实验室设置中验证，而完整显微镜台具有耦合非线性运动学且缺乏专用POI传感器，因此需要新方法。

Method: 该框架包括：逐元件迟滞补偿以线性化执行器；使用交叉相关图像跟踪从EM图像构建POI测量；基于编码器构建POI代理；在换向角域应用迭代学习控制以消除可重复的步进扰动，因参数化于换向角，可跨准静态频率范围转移。

Result: 在实验室设置中POI跟踪误差降低超过13倍，在操作中的透射电子显微镜上降低7到12倍。

Conclusion: 该框架在缺乏专用POI传感器的实际电子显微镜台上有效提高了定位精度，显著减少了跟踪误差，证明了其在实际应用中的可行性。

Abstract: Electron microscopy requires nanometer-scale specimen positioning over a long stroke. Piezo-stepper actuators are well suited for this task, but their accuracy is limited by hysteresis, mechanical misalignments, and non-collocated sensing. Prior work has addressed these limitations on simplified lab setups. However, extending to a full electron microscope stage introduces coupled nonlinear kinematics and, importantly, the absence of a dedicated point-of-interest (POI) sensor. This paper presents an integrated feedforward framework for precision positioning on such a stage inside an operational electron microscope. Per-element hysteresis compensation first linearizes the actuator response. In the absence of a dedicated POI sensor, a POI measurement is constructed from EM images through cross-correlation-based image tracking. From this measurement, we construct an encoder-based proxy for the POI position. Commutation-angle-domain iterative learning control then uses this proxy as its error signal to cancel the repeatable disturbances of stepping. Because the learned corrections are parameterized in the commutation angle, they transfer across the quasi-static range of drive frequencies. The framework reduces the POI tracking error by over 13x on the lab setup and by 7 to 12x on an operational transmission electron microscope.

</details>


### [385] [A Conductance Based Amygdala Model of Threat Processing in Anxiety and Depression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03712)
*Malik Faizan,P. J. White,Indrakshi Dey*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于杏仁核-下丘脑-心血管通路的紧凑机制模型，该模型将心理调节因素（应对能力、感知压力负荷、前额叶调控强度）与心率、血压的动态变化联系起来，并通过模拟和外部数据验证，为数字表型分析和精神健康决策支持提供了可解释的计算框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 焦虑和抑郁障碍被视为压力调节维度的失调，但现有计算方法很少将可解释的回路机制与自主神经生理学联系起来，缺乏能够连接心理调节因素、杏仁核兴奋性和心血管输出的紧凑模型。

Method: 构建了一个包含九个方程的基于电导的模型，扩展Hodgkin-Huxley形式，引入三种临床调节因子，并包括缓慢历史依赖的内部状态、自适应阈值、分级威胁识别和与压力感受器耦合的下丘脑整合，以生成心率和血压轨迹。

Result: 模型出现强、中、弱三种稳定调节状态，鲁棒性分析表明随机变异和参数扰动不会破坏状态分离，消融实验显示自适应阈值是状态分离的主要机制。模拟结果符合压力生理范围，并在三个独立数据集上验证了与实际压力相关自主神经模式的一致性。

Conclusion: 该紧凑机制模型能够在单一可解释框架内联合心理调节因素、杏仁核兴奋性和心血管输出，为机制信息驱动的数字表型分析、个性化压力监测和未来心理健康数字孪生应用提供了计算基础。

Abstract: Anxiety and depressive disorders are increasingly viewed as dysregulations along continuous stress-regulatory dimensions. However, existing computational approaches seldom connect interpretable circuit level mechanisms to autonomic physiology. Methods: This study develops a mechanistic framework that links amygdala dysregulation to cardiovascular stress responses for digital phenotyping and clinical interpretation. We formulated a compact, nine equation, conductance based model of the amygdala hypothalamus cardiovascular pathway. The framework extends Hodgkin Huxley formalism with three clinically grounded modulators: coping capacity, perceived stress load, and prefrontal regulatory strength. A slow, history-dependent internal state, adaptive thresholding, graded threat acknowledgement, and baroreflex coupled hypothalamic integration were used to generate heart rate and blood pressure trajectories. Results: Distinct strong, moderate, and weak regulatory regimes emerged as stable operating states of a single closed-loop system. Robust analyses showed that stochastic variability and parameter perturbation preserved regime separation, while ablation studies identified the adaptive threshold as the principal mechanism driving quantitative regime separation. Simulated cardiovascular responses remained within reported stress physiology ranges. Furthermore, external evaluation across three independent datasets supported robust agreement with real world, stress related autonomic patterns. Conclusion: A compact, mechanistic model can jointly link psychometric modulators, amygdala excitability, and downstream cardiovascular output within a single, interpretable framework. Significance: This work provides a computationally tractable basis for mechanism informed digital phenotyping, patient specific stress monitoring, and future digital twin approaches for mental health decision support.

</details>


### [386] [Input-to-State Stability of Reset-Integral Sliding Mode Control for Linear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03802)
*Xinxin Zhang,Leonid Freidovich*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文对集成单重置状态重置控制器与积分滑模控制器的混合控制系统进行了稳定性分析，证明了滑模面可达性与标称重置机制解耦，并基于李雅普诺夫方法建立了系统的输入-状态稳定性和一致最终有界性，以及在无扰动调节情况下的全局渐近稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对重置控制器与积分滑模控制器组合系统的严格稳定性分析，而此类混合控制具有潜在优势，需要理论支撑以保证其在实际应用中的可靠性。

Method: 通过理论推导证明滑模面可达性与重置机制的解耦性质，并利用李雅普诺夫理论分析闭环系统的稳定性，包括输入-状态稳定性、一致最终有界性和全局渐近稳定性。

Result: 闭环RC-ISMC系统在存在外部参考和扰动时满足输入-状态稳定性和一致最终有界性；在无扰动调节情况下恢复全局渐近稳定性。数值案例验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合控制结构在保证稳定性的同时具有抗扰动能力，为重置控制与滑模控制的结合提供了理论依据，并有望扩展至更复杂的控制场景。

Abstract: This work presents a stability analysis of a hybrid control system integrating a reset controller (RC) featuring a single reset state with an integral sliding-mode controller (ISMC). It is shown that the reachability of the sliding surface is decoupled from the nominal reset mechanism. This decoupling property enables a Lyapunov-based stability analysis, demonstrating that the closed-loop RC-ISMC system achieves input-to-state stability (ISS) and uniform ultimate boundedness (UUB) in the presence of exogenous references and disturbances. Furthermore, global asymptotic stability (GAS) is recovered in the unperturbed regulation scenario. A numerical case study illustrates the theoretical results.

</details>


### [387] [A Stackelberg-Bayesian Capacity-Market Game of Carbon Regulation and Second-Life Battery Investment under AI Data-Center Load Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03989)
*Rouzbeh Haghighi,Ali Hassan,Sina Mohammadi,Marcus Chen I Wada,Wencong Su*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个三层Stackelberg-Bayesian博弈模型，分析AI数据中心电力需求增长对电网容量扩张和碳排放的影响，并评估碳税、可再生能源补贴及二手电池储能补贴等政策工具的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI数据中心的快速扩张导致美国各ISO/RTO区域电力负荷激增，推高系统成本和碳排放，亟需量化其影响并设计有效的激励政策以引导投资向低碳技术倾斜。

Method: 采用三层博弈框架：监管者（领导者）设定碳惩罚和补贴；ISO容量市场基于经典发电扩展问题清算；技术投资者（追随者）在信息不完整条件下决策，形成贝叶斯纳什均衡。将AI负荷增长作为额外因子纳入绿地增量扩展模型，并引入二手电池储能与新型储能的竞争。

Result: 量化了碳税、可再生能源补贴和二手电池补贴对均衡投资组合、碳排放和利润的影响，并基于成本效益和减排效果对多种情景进行了比较。

Conclusion: 研究表明，政策组合（如碳税与补贴结合）能显著重塑投资结构，减少碳排放，其中二手电池储能在特定政策下具有成本效益优势，为应对AI驱动的电力需求增长提供了决策参考。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) data centers are driving rapid electricity load growth across all U.S. ISO/RTO regions, raising both system costs and carbon exposure. This study develops a three-level Stackelberg--Bayesian game in which a regulator (leader) sets carbon penalties and subsidies, a single ISO capacity market clears against an energy balance modeled as a classical generation-expansion problem, and technology-specific investors (followers) decide capacity and operation under incomplete information, yielding a Bayesian Nash equilibrium. The AI impact is captured parsimoniously as an additional load-growth factor on a greenfield-incremental expansion, isolating how much new capacity the growth pulls in and which technology fills it. Within this framework, we consider second-life battery (SLB) storage competing against new/first-life storage for capacity-market revenue. We quantify how a carbon tax, a renewable subsidy, and an SLB subsidy reshape the equilibrium investment mix, carbon emissions, and profit. Different scenarios are compared at the end based on cost-effectiveness and reduced carbon emissions.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [388] [Automatic Patient-Specific Microwave Ablation Planning Accelerated by a Physics-Guided Deep Learning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03086)
*Seonaeng Cho,Minjee Seo,Minju Seol,Juil Park,Joon Ho Kwon,Kyungho Yoon*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一个基于数字孪生的微波消融自动规划框架，结合神经网络预测模型和遗传算法，实现快速、个性化的治疗规划，显著提高效率并减少器官损伤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波消融治疗效果依赖个体化规划，但数值模拟计算成本高，难以用于优化迭代。

Method: 使用多物理场仿真数据训练神经网络预测模型作为快速前向模型，并与遗传算法结合进行自动规划。

Result: Dice分数95.1%，规划效率提高54.3%，器官损伤减少55.0%，路径长度缩短3.3%，规划速度比数值模拟快420倍。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为快速数字孪生，支持定量和个性化的微波消融治疗规划，具有临床适用性。

Abstract: Microwave ablation (MWA) is a promising minimally invasive treatment for liver tumors, but its therapeutic outcome strongly depends on patient-specific planning of antenna insertion trajectory, power, and treatment duration. Accurate numerical simulation can provide physically reliable ablation predictions; however, its high computational cost limits its use in optimization-based planning, where repeated forward evaluations are required. To address this issue, we propose a digital twin-based automatic planning framework that combines a neural ablation prediction model with a genetic algorithm. The model was trained on multiphysics simulation data generated from patient-specific tumor and vessel structures, antenna configurations, and treatment conditions, and was used as a fast forward model during planning. The prediction model achieved a Dice score of 95.1%, enabling accurate deep learning-based optimization. In 13 unseen planning cases, the proposed method improved ablation efficiency by 54.3% and reduced organ damage by 55.0% compared with clinician-defined planning, while slightly shortening the insertion path length by 3.3%. Most generated plans were also judged clinically applicable by MWA specialists. Furthermore, the framework enabled approximately 420-fold faster planning than numerical-simulation-based planning, demonstrating its potential as a fast digital twin for quantitative and personalized MWA treatment planning. The code is available at: https://github.com/SeonAengCho/MWA-Planning.git

</details>


### [389] [ScoreField: Neural Inverse Scattering with Score-Based Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02937)
*Wenhan Guo,Yuan Gao,Yu Sun*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出ScoreField，一种结合隐式神经表示与基于分数的生成先验的电磁逆散射框架，通过Lippmann-Schwinger方程联合优化两个INR，有效处理强多重散射，在模拟和实验数据上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电磁逆散射求解器难以同时满足非线性全波物理和表达性先验，尤其是在强散射场景下，需要更有效的建模方法。

Method: 使用两个耦合的隐式神经表示分别参数化介电常数对比度和感应电流场，并在Lippmann-Schwinger方程约束下联合优化；同时，利用预训练的基于分数的生成先验提供对比度的梯度，通过链式法则传播到对比度INR。

Result: 在模拟弱/强散射基准、奥地利体模和实验Fresnel数据上，ScoreField显著提升重建保真度，抑制伪影，在真实Fresnel数据上平均PSNR比最佳对比方法高1.8 dB。

Conclusion: ScoreField通过结合INR隐式正则化和学习先验，能够有效处理强多重散射，提升逆散射重建质量。

Abstract: Designing an effective electromagnetic inverse-scattering solver requires faithful enforcement of nonlinear full-wave physics together with an expressive prior on the unknown permittivity contrast. We propose ScoreField, a neural inverse scattering framework that integrates coupled implicit neural representations (INRs) with a pretrained score-based generative prior. ScoreField employs two INRs to parameterize the permittivity contrast and the induced current fields, and jointly optimize them under the Lippmann-Schwinger equations. In addition to the implicit regularization by the INR architecture, the score model provides a learned prior gradient on the contrast, which is propagated to the contrast INR through the chain rule. This formulation enables ScoreField to effectively handle strong multiple scattering, where nonlinear wave interactions require accurate modeling of the coupled full-wave physics. We evaluate ScoreField on simulated weak- and strong-scattering benchmarks, the canonical Austria phantom, and experimental Fresnel measurements. We note that ScoreField significantly improves reconstruction fidelity and suppresses artifacts relative to classical full-wave methods and deep learning baselines, achieving an average PSNR improvement of $1.8 \, \mathrm{dB}$ over the best competing method on real Fresnel data.

</details>


### [390] [Radiometric Thermal Imaging Dataset of Laboratory Rats with Anatomical Segmentation Masks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03481)
*Dima Bykhovsky,Evyatar Chaimoff,Pe'er Eden,Tom Simkin,Oshrit Hoffer,Shahar Cohen,Bar Eilat Yogev,Gal Levi,Noa Efroni,Doron Todder,Hagit Cohen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含1,655帧大鼠热红外图像的数据集，每帧配有像素级解剖分割标签（头、体、尾）和温度矩阵，并展示了基于U-Net的分割基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热红外成像在动物应激和药理研究中很有价值，但图像分析受限于手动标注解剖区域，且缺乏公开的配对数据集。

Method: 构建了包含25只大鼠的1,655帧热红外数据集，每帧提供四类像素级分割掩膜和原始温度矩阵；并提出一个探索性的U-Net分割流程作为基线。

Result: U-Net在受试者交叉验证中达到平均IoU 0.895±0.006；数据集的每类温度符合头>体>尾的顺序。

Conclusion: 该数据集为热红外语义分割和生理/应激表型分析提供了可复用的基准，支持直接基于热辐射通道进行像素级分割。

Abstract: Infrared thermography provides a contact-free, restraint-free method to record surface temperatures. It serves as a valuable marker for thermoregulatory responses in laboratory animal stress and pharmacology research. However, the analysis of these images is currently bottlenecked by the manual delineation of anatomical regions. To date, no public dataset has provided paired radiometric thermal frames of rats with pixel-level body-part labels. We present a dataset of 1,655 quality-controlled radiometric thermal frames from 25 laboratory rats. Each frame is paired with a dense four-class anatomical segmentation mask (background, head, body, and tail) and the raw $480 \times 640$ temperature matrix (rows $\times$ columns) in degrees Celsius. This ensures every label is registered directly to the physical temperature it describes rather than a color-mapped rendering. The frames originate from two pharmacological cohorts where interventions alter thermoregulation in opposite directions: ethanol, which induces peripheral vasodilation, and ketamine, which affects central thermoregulation. This provides a wide and physiologically diverse range of surface temperature regimes. Aggregated across the dataset, the per-class temperatures follow a head~$>$~body~$>$~tail ordering in physical units. To demonstrate that the data support pixel-level segmentation directly from the radiometric channel, we present an exploratory U-Net segmentation pipeline that attains a subject-level cross-validated mean intersection-over-union of $0.895 \pm 0.006$. The dataset provides a reuse-ready benchmark for thermal semantic segmentation and for downstream physiological and stress-phenotyping analyses.

</details>


### [391] [Predictive Enhancement Calibration for Latent Breast MRI Virtual Contrast Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03612)
*Qin Lei,Hao Wu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出预测增强校准（PEC）方法，通过共享案例自适应坐标系解决MRI强度尺度与预训练潜在生成器不匹配的问题，实现源图像到虚拟增强图像的生成，并在内部数据集上提升所有评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在生成器虽提供强大图像先验，但其自然图像自编码器的有界坐标与MRI非标准强度尺度冲突，导致放射组学保真度下降；独立缩放源和目标图像会引入坐标不一致性。

Method: PEC在训练时将每对图像表示在共享案例自适应坐标中，推理时从预对比图像预测不可得的增强图像上端点；集成预训练FLUX潜在流变换器，采用参数高效的参考条件化，并设计目标往返测试分离表示损失与生成损失。

Result: 在内部MAMA100开发队列中，PEC在所有八项点估计上优于固定宽坐标和独立坐标方法，其中MSE和LPIPS的配对证据最显著。

Conclusion: PEC有效解决了MRI强度尺度与潜在生成器的兼容性问题，提升了虚拟增强图像的生成质量，代码已公开。

Abstract: Virtual contrast enhancement (VCE) synthesizes enhanced breast MR images from pre-contrast acquisitions. Modern latent generators offer strong image priors, but their bounded natural-image autoencoders conflict with the non-canonical intensity scale of MRI. We show that the upper bound can alter radiomic fidelity before generation, while scaling source and target independently creates a coordinate inconsistency. We propose Predictive Enhancement Calibration (PEC), which represents each pair in a shared, case-adaptive coordinate during training and predicts its unavailable upper endpoint from the pre-contrast image at inference. We integrate PEC with a pretrained FLUX latent flow transformer via parameter-efficient reference conditioning. Target round trips first isolate representation loss before generation; near-matched conditional models then compare PEC with fixed-wide and separate coordinates under comparable training budgets and backbone settings. On the fixed internal MAMA100 development cohort, PEC improves all eight point estimates in this source-only VCE setting, with paired evidence strongest for MSE and LPIPS.\noindent\textbf{Code:} https://github.com/tanlei0/pec-breast-mri-vce

</details>


### [392] [Unsupervised Adversarial Domain Adaptation for Uterine layer Segmentation: From Labeled Cine to Unlabeled Dynamic EPI MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03762)
*Smiti Tripathy,Milauni Desai,Jordina Aviles Verdera,Jana Hutter*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在0.55T下动态多回波梯度回波EPI框架，结合无监督对抗域自适应分割方法，同时表征子宫蠕动活动和T2*动态变化，并首次揭示交界区收缩与氧合模式的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子宫蠕动与子宫壁微结构密切相关，但以往研究将子宫运动和组织特性分开研究，缺乏同时评估两者相互作用的手段。

Method: 采用动态多回波梯度回波EPI成像，结合无监督对抗域自适应框架（Unet-LSTM与多尺度域判别器），将标记的cine MRI分割知识迁移至未标记的动态EPI数据。

Result: 分割Dice系数0.88，Jaccard指数0.80；子宫肌层、交界区、内膜的平均T2*分别为108ms、76ms和124ms；39例中14例观察到交界区面积与T2*负相关。

Conclusion: 该框架可行地同时评估子宫蠕动与动态T2*变化，为研究交界区收缩与氧合模式的关系提供了新见解。

Abstract: Uterine peristalsis is a key physiological phenomenon responsible for various functions across the menstrual cycle, intimately linked to uterine wall microstructure. Alterations in uterine motion and tissue properties are implicated in the etiology of gynecological diseases, yet these processes have been studied in isolation. We introduce a dynamic multi-echo gradient echo EPI framework for simultaneous characterization and correlation of uterine peristaltic activity and time-resolved T2* changes at 0.55T. Inherent susceptibility artifacts, reduced resolution, and burden of manual uterine layer annotation are addressed by an unsupervised adversarial domain adaptation framework, transferring segmentation knowledge from labeled cine MRI to unlabeled dynamic EPI. We implemented Unet-LSTM with multi-scale domain discriminators that exploits temporal layer dynamics. A Dice score of 0.88 and Jaccard index of 0.80 was achieved. Mean T2* values were 108ms, 76ms, and 124ms for the myometrium, junctional zone, and endometrium. A negative correlation between junctional zone area and T2* was observed in 14/39 cases, providing first insights into oxygenation patterns associated with junctional zone contraction and motion, demonstrating feasibility of assessing the interplay between contractility and dynamic T2* changes.

</details>
